Extensive experiments across three MoE-based LLMs and eight benchmarks demonstrate that ACE consistently outperforms existing static and dynamic baselines, with increasingly pronounced advantages under aggressive expert skipping. For instance, at a 50% skipping ratio on Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, ACE reduces WikiText-2 perplexity by 7.96% and improves average downstream accuracy by 4.15 percentage points over the strongest competing method.

</details>


### [234] [Substrate-Aware AI Agents: Execution Context as a First-Class Input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05232)
*Manu Agrawal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"基底盲区"（substrate blindness）概念——自主AI代理在规划时缺乏执行环境（内存、时间、计算等约束）的上下文。通过数值代码生成任务验证：向前沿模型披露128MB RAM和10秒墙钟时间的执行契约后，生成代码的峰值内存和运行时间显著降低，最高提速3.1倍；在更严格的96MB约束下，符合预算的成功率从0-1/5提升至3-5/5。实验证明了最小执行契约可诱导代理产生主动的结构性代码适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI代理在选择动作时往往忽略了运行环境的实际约束（内存、执行时间、计算、运行时等），这些约束决定了什么样的计划才切实可行。作者将这种执行上下文缺失称为"基底盲区"（substrate blindness），并希望通过受控实验验证：若向代理提供执行契约，是否能促使其生成更符合资源约束的方案。

Method: 以数值代码生成为测试平台（因实现选择和运行后果可直接观测）。使用三个前沿模型配置（Anthropic Claude Opus 5、OpenAI GPT-5.6-Sol、Google Gemini 3.7 Flash）生成高维成对欧氏距离任务的代码。对比两种条件：(1)仅给任务描述；(2)附带128MB RAM和10秒墙钟时间的执行契约。测量峰值进程内存（MaxRSS）和墙钟时间。随后在更紧的96MB契约下进行独立采样重复验证。

Result: 契约披露在14个可执行索引对齐对比中有13个降低了峰值内存，三个队列的平均墙钟时间均下降，执行速度最高提升3.1倍。代码审计显示披露契约诱发了结构性改变：有界阻塞、float32保留、上三角遍历、原地或内存映射缓冲。在96MB更紧契约下，三模型契约披露组的正确且在预算内成功率分别为4/5、5/5、3/5，而任务仅描述组为0/5、1/5、0/5；队列平均MaxRSS和墙钟时间分别比基线低49-74%和35-64%。

Conclusion: 实验建立了一个受控的概念验证：基底感知式代理规划（substrate-aware agent planning）——即一个最小执行契约能诱导生成程序产生主动的结构性适配，使计算从无约束的内存分配中转移出来，在程序执行前就显著改善观测到的资源-时间特征。这为未来在代理规划中纳入执行上下文提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents increasingly select actions in environments whose memory, execution-time, runtime, compute, and operational constraints determine what counts as a suitable plan. We call the absence of this execution context from an agent's planning state substrate blindness. We test this general proposition through numerical code generation, where selected implementation choices and operational consequences are directly observable. Three frontier model configurations--Anthropic Claude Opus 5, OpenAI GPT-5.6-Sol, and Google Gemini 3.7 Flash--generate code for a high-dimensional pairwise Euclidean-distance task either from the task alone or with a 128 MB RAM and 10.0 s wall-time contract. Contract disclosure reduced measured peak process memory in 13 of 14 executable index-aligned task-only versus contract-disclosed comparisons and reduced mean wall time in all three cohorts, making execution up to 3.1x faster. Across the audited corpus, disclosure produced structural code changes including bounded blocking, float32 retention, upper-triangle traversal, and in-place or memory-mapped buffers. At a tighter 96 MB contract, independently sampled contract-disclosed cohorts achieved correct-and-within-budget outcomes of 4/5 for Claude Opus 5, 5/5 for GPT-5.6-Sol, and 3/5 for Gemini 3.7 Flash, compared with task-only outcomes of 0/5, 1/5, and 0/5; cohort mean MaxRSS and wall time were 49-74% and 35-64% lower than their task-only references. These results establish a controlled proof of concept for substrate-aware agent planning: a minimal execution contract induces proactive structural adaptation in generated programs, shifting computation away from unconstrained allocations and substantially improving observed resource-time profiles before execution.

</details>


### [235] [Uncensored Open-weight Models: Redistribution as the Persistence Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05241)
*10a Labs,:,Juliette Garcia,Hailey May,Bobby McKenzie,David Pham,Matthew Swain,Joshua Valdez,Corie Wieland,Zachary Yahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对去除安全护栏的开源AI模型生态系统进行了全面画像，识别了关键生产者和下游复制传播模式。2024年1月至2026年3月间，在HuggingFace上发现3,471个原始"无审查"模型，平均每个被重新打包2.4次；三家参与者占据了8,164次压缩再分发中的52%。量化压缩后的模型在多个账户、格式和注册表（如Ollama）中镜像持久存在，即使上游删除也难以消除。在1,643个集成这些模型的GitHub应用中，25%被判定为明确恶意。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示去除安全护栏的开源AI模型这一快速扩张生态系统的结构特征，包括关键生产者、再生产模式和恶意应用，以引起对开源模型安全治理和防护措施失效问题的关注。

Method: 通过系统化的大规模实证研究，对HuggingFace上的模型进行识别与追踪（2024年1月至2026年3月），分析模型的原始分布、重新打包、量化压缩和跨注册表镜像传播路径；同时对GitHub上集成这些模型的应用程序进行分类评估，判定其是否具有恶意性质。

Result: 识别出3,471个原始无审查模型，平均每个被重新打包2.4次，累计8,164次压缩再分发，其中52%由三家核心参与者完成。发现模型可通过量化压缩和多平台（账户、格式、注册表如Ollama）镜像实现持久化和更易部署。在1,643个GitHub应用中，25%被明确分类为恶意。

Conclusion: 去护栏开源模型的生态系统具有高度集中化的生产结构（寡头生产、广泛复制）与广泛扩散的恶意应用并存的特征；由于量化压缩和多平台持久性机制，即使上游模型被删除，已发布的实体仍难以从生态系统中移除，对现有监管和治理框架构成严峻挑战。

Abstract: A rapidly expanding ecosystem of actors is removing built-in safety guardrails from open-weight AI models. We profile this ecosystem by identifying key producers, downstream reproductions, and emerging applications. Between January 2024 and March 2026, we identified 3,471 original uncensored models on HuggingFace, each repackaged an average of 2.4 times; three actors account for 52% of all 8,164 compressed redistributions. Once quantized and mirrored across separate accounts, formats, and registries such as Ollama, these models persist regardless of upstream removal and become easier to deploy downstream. Of the 1,643 identified GitHub applications integrating uncensored large language models (ULLMs), 25% were classified as explicitly malicious.

</details>


### [236] [Do LLMs Exhibit Coherent Knowledge Structures in Mathematical Reasoning? A Perspective from Knowledge Space Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05245)
*Peng Cui,Heejin Do,Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于知识空间理论（KST）提出一个评估框架，用于分析LLM在数学推理中是否展现出类似人类的知识结构。实验表明LLM既不遵循人类的知识依赖关系，LLM之间也不共享一致的知识结构，且这些结构性缺陷在传统准确率评估和LLM-as-judge评估中几乎不可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类知识天然具有结构性和相互依赖性，掌握一个概念需要先掌握其前提知识（由KST形式化）。虽然LLM在复杂推理任务上表现优异，但尚不清楚它们是否具备连贯的、类人的知识结构。现有评估主要关注正确率而忽视知识组织的结构性，因此需要一个规范性的框架来检验LLM行为是否符合原则性的知识依赖关系。

Method: 引入KST作为规范性框架来评估LLM的数学推理知识结构。对8个开源和闭源LLM与真实人类学习者进行对比评估，考察两个核心维度：(1) LLM是否遵循人类知识依赖关系——即在上下文中能否利用相关知识提升对依赖问题的表现；(2) LLM之间是否共享一致的知识结构——通过其知识分布的重叠程度来衡量。

Result: 实验发现：(1) LLM不遵循人类知识结构，频繁违反知识依赖关系，且无法利用上下文中提供的相关知识来改进对依赖问题的回答；(2) 不同LLM之间缺乏一致的知识结构，其知识分布重叠度低。此外，这些结构性缺陷在传统的准确率基线和LLM-as-judge评估中基本无法被察觉。

Conclusion: 当前LLM的知识并不遵循类人的结构化的知识组织方式。研究提供了行为层面的证据，表明LLM在数学推理中缺乏人类式的知识依赖结构，并指出了传统评估指标的局限性，强调了结构性评估（如基于KST的框架）对于全面理解LLM能力的重要性。

Abstract: Human knowledge is inherently structured and interdependent: mastery of a concept requires prior mastery of its prerequisites, a principle formalized by Knowledge Space Theory (KST). While LLMs achieve strong performance on complex reasoning tasks, it remains unclear whether they exhibit coherent, human-like knowledge structure. We introduce a KST-grounded framework for evaluating LLM knowledge structure in mathematical reasoning, using it as a normative framework to analyze whether LLM behavior adheres to principled knowledge dependencies. Evaluating eight open- and closed-source LLMs against real human learners, we find that (1) LLMs do not adhere to human knowledge structure -- they frequently violate knowledge dependencies and fail to leverage related knowledge provided in context to improve performance on dependent questions; (2) LLMs do not share a consistent knowledge structure among themselves, as reflected by low overlap in their knowledge distributions. Furthermore, these structural deficiencies remain largely invisible to accuracy-based and LLM-as-judge evaluations. Together, our results provide behavioral evidence that current LLMs knowledge does not follow a human-like structure.

</details>


### [237] [A Unified Physics-Aware Quantum Machine Learning Framework across Power GaN HEMTs and Logic Nanowire FETs: Predicting Unseen Process Splits and Held-Out Geometry Combinations with Lower Error and Tighter Split-to-Split Variability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05251)
*Rushat Rai,Yun-Yuan Wang,Autsada Kakaen,Pei-Jie Chang,Doan Viet Nguyen,Yuan-Chieh Chiu,Doldet Tantraviwat,Niall Tumilty,Simon See,Wen-Jay Lee,Tai-Yue Li,Nan-Yow Chen,Tian-Li Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的强化学习框架，利用图神经网络策略和近端策略优化（PPO）搜索紧凑的参数化量子电路（PQC），用于数据稀缺的器件建模。该框架在11个目标上均优于六个经典基线，在HEMT和NWFET器件上分别实现了59%-84%的误差降低和81%-82%的折叠变异度收紧，展示出RL选择的PQC作为低分布外（OOD）误差的紧凑替代模型潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据稀缺场景下的半导体器件建模面临挑战，传统经典模型和人工设计的量子电路难以在少量数据下实现高精度和物理一致性，急需一个无需显式物理约束即可自动发现紧凑替代模型的统一框架。

Method: 采用统一的强化学习框架：以图神经网络为策略，通过近端策略优化（PPO）进行优化；以留一组交叉验证（LOGOCV）在保留的工艺或几何组上的误差作为奖励信号，搜索紧凑PQC的电路架构。

Result: 在所有11个目标上取得最低平均绝对误差（MAE），优于六个经典基线。对HEMT器件，Ioff误差降低59%，VTH折叠变异度收紧81%；对NWFET器件，VTH、SS、Ioff误差降低84%，Ioff折叠变异度收紧82%。

Conclusion: RL选择并在经典计算机上模拟的PQC可作为低OOD误差、物理一致性改善的紧凑替代模型，且无需施加显式物理约束、惩罚项或器件专用方程。

Abstract: We present a unified reinforcement-learning (RL) framework that discovers compact parametrized quantum circuits (PQCs) for data-scarce device modeling. A graph neural network (GNN) policy optimized by proximal policy optimization (PPO) searches circuit architectures using leave-one-group-out cross-validation (LOGOCV) error on held-out process or geometry groups as the reward. The framework achieves the lowest mean absolute error (MAE) on all 11 targets versus six classical baselines, with 59% lower error (Ioff) and 81% tighter fold variability (VTH) for HEMTs and 84% lower error (VTH, SS, Ioff) and 82% tighter fold variability (Ioff) for NWFETs. These results demonstrate the potential of RL-selected, classically simulated PQCs as compact surrogates with low OOD error and improved physical consistency, despite imposing no explicit physical constraints, penalty terms, or device-specific equations, on the two evaluated device datasets.

</details>


### [238] [Commonsense Reasoning in Computer Vision: Foundations, Recent Advancements, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05257)
*Bahar Uddin Mahmud,Sumit Barua,Guan Yue Hong,Ajay Gupta,Hexu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述论文，系统梳理了将常识知识融入计算机视觉任务的最新进展。论文回顾了基于知识图谱、场景图、神经符号模型和常识增强Transformer的四类方法，指出了数据集偏差、知识不完整和集成挑战等当前局限，并展望了跨模态推理、可扩展常识知识注入及神经符号混合架构等未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于CNN的视觉模型仅能识别图像中的特定对象，缺乏对场景整体性和对象间关系的理解能力。将常识知识融入视觉任务可使模型以更整体的方式理解场景，提升空间推理和上下文感知能力，从而在真实应用中实现更精确的预测和交互。作者旨在通过系统综述推动这一领域的发展。

Method: 作者采用综述方法，系统性地回顾并分类整理了近年将常识知识集成到计算机视觉任务中的方法，将其划分为四个主要类别：基于知识图谱的方法、基于场景图的方法、神经符号模型以及常识增强的Transformer架构，并对每种方法进行了系统性梳理与归纳。

Result: 论文梳理了四类主流集成方案及其在增强视觉常识推理方面的进展，同时识别出三大核心挑战：数据集偏差（训练数据中存在的偏见影响泛化）、知识不完整性（常识知识库覆盖有限）以及集成难度（常识知识与视觉特征的有效融合仍具挑战）。

Conclusion: 论文展望了三个未来研究方向：跨模态推理、可扩展的常识知识注入方法，以及神经符号混合架构，认为这些方向是构建真正智能视觉系统的关键路径。

Abstract: Commonsense reasoning in computer vision encompasses integrating visual data and contextual knowledge, crucial for enhancing AI's understanding of everyday scenarios. This understanding not only improves machine learning models but also enhances their ability to interact meaningfully with humans and the environment. Unlike CNN-based conventional vision models, which are designed to identify objects within a specific image, incorporating commonsense knowledge enables models to interpret scenes in a more holistic manner, thereby improving their spatial ability to reason about relationships among objects and actions. This integration not only enhances object recognition but also facilitates a deeper understanding of the contextual factors, ultimately leading to more precise predictions and interactions in real-world applications. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of recent developments that integrate commonsense knowledge into computer vision tasks. We systematically review approaches based on knowledge graphs, scene graphs, neuro-symbolic models, and commonsense-augmented transformers. We also outline current limitations related to dataset bias, knowledge incompleteness, and integration challenges. Finally, we highlight prospective research trajectories in cross-modal reasoning, scalable commonsense knowledge injection, and neuro-symbolic hybrid architectures to develop truly intelligent visual systems.

</details>


### [239] [Trace2Tower: Transition-Aware EigenTrace Induction of Multi-Level Skills for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05261)
*Jiazheng Sun,Boyu Yang,Binhao Yuan,Mingxuan Li,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Trace2Tower是一个基于转换感知的EigenTrace框架，通过将原始轨迹蒸馏为规范的技能层级结构，利用对比谱分解提取与成功对齐的行为模式，在ALFWorld和WebShop上显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型智能体在执行轨迹利用上存在两个根本瓶颈：轨迹检索过于浅层、技能归纳过于扁平化，忽略了智能体行为中蕴含的时间依赖和结果条件化的拓扑结构。这种浅层范式限制了智能体在复杂交互任务中的经验复用效率。

Method: Trace2Tower包含以下核心步骤：(1) 将步骤级别的交互抽象为规范事件，构建统一图结构，该图由语义兼容性、转换动态和结果证据共同约束；(2) 通过新颖的对比谱分解技术，从图中分离出稳定且对齐成功的行为模式，同时抑制容易失败的捷径行为；(3) 将提取的行为模式组织成动态技能塔，包含动作模板、程序例程和任务级策略三个层次；(4) 通过验证器引导的反馈机制持续优化技能层级。

Result: 在ALFWorld上，Trace2Tower达到87.31%的成功率，平均仅需10.35步和0.26个无效动作；在WebShop上达到50.67%的精确成功率。在两个基准测试中均显著优于现有基线。

Conclusion: Trace2Tower通过转换感知的谱分解和层级化技能组织，验证了结果条件化的行为拓扑对智能体经验高效复用和任务掌握的关键性作用，展示了在复杂交互任务中超越现有方法的能力。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on execution traces to master complex interactive tasks. However, current paradigms are bottlenecked by shallow trajectory retrieval and flat skill summarization, fundamentally ignoring the temporal dependencies and outcome-conditioned topology of agent behavior. We introduce Trace2Tower, a transition-aware EigenTrace framework that distills raw trajectories into a robust skill hierarchy. Trace2Tower abstracts step-level interactions into canonical events, constructing a unified graph governed by semantic compatibility, transition dynamics, and outcome evidence. Through a novel contrastive spectral decomposition, it isolates stable, success-aligned behavioral modes while rigorously suppressing failure-prone shortcuts. These modes organically populate a dynamic skill tower of action templates, procedural routines, and overarching task strategies, continuously refined via verifier-guided feedback. On ALFWorld, Trace2Tower achieves 87.31% success requiring only 10.35 steps and 0.26 invalid actions; on WebShop, it reaches 50.67% exact success. Across both benchmarks, Trace2Tower significantly outperforms existing baselines in task mastery and context-efficient experience reuse.

</details>


### [240] [AI for Computational Design Science: A Responsible Human-AI Framework and Case Study on Short-Form Video Safety Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05270)
*Wenli Zhang,Jiaheng Xie,Zhihe Pan,Yidong Chai,Xiao Fang,Sudha Ram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI4CDS框架——一个五阶段方法框架，支持AI深度参与计算设计科学(CDS)研究，并通过可解释的儿童短视频风险检测系统ChildRiskGuard进行实例化验证。AI负责扩展问题与设计搜索空间，研究者主导领域基础、可接受性、验证与科学判断，以分级信任、可逆性、可审计性和差异化可复现性为协作治理原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI正在改变信息系统研究中"如何设计"的方式，但现有文献缺乏当AI主动参与问题表述、资源配置、设计搜索、评估和知识抽象时对计算设计科学(CDS)的指导，存在方法论空白。

Method: 提出AI4CDS五阶段方法框架：AI扩展问题与设计搜索空间，研究者保留领域接地、可接受性、验证和科学判断的责任；协作由分级信任、可逆性、可审计性和差异化可复现性治理。通过ChildRiskGuard实例化：将受众依赖的安全性和解释忠实度转化为三个技术挑战，开发了能分离通用风险与儿童特定风险、表征不同发展风险机制、并将概念级解释融入预测计算的构件。

Result: ChildRiskGuard在F1得分上达到0.769，显著优于直接应用通用内容安全模型，并与强基准保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是AI4CDS作为AI赋能CDS的责任框架；ChildRiskGuard提供了过程与构件证据，展示AI扩展、研究者治理的设计如何生成和评估新颖的计算设计知识。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is transforming not only what information systems researchers design, but also how design research is conducted. Yet existing literature offers limited guidance for computational design science (CDS) when AI actively participates in problem formulation, resource construction, design search, evaluation, and knowledge abstraction. We develop AI for Computational Design Science (AI4CDS), a five-phase methodological framework in which AI expands problem and design search while researchers retain responsibility for domain grounding, admissibility, verification, and scientific judgment. Collaboration is governed by graduated trust, reversibility, auditability, and differentiated reproducibility. We instantiate AI4CDS through ChildRiskGuard, an interpretable artifact for detecting short-form videos inappropriate for children, while documenting AI interactions, rejected alternatives, corrections, and audit trails. The case translates audience-dependent safety and explanation faithfulness into three technical challenges and develops an artifact that separates generic from child-specific risk, represents distinct developmental-risk mechanisms, and makes concept-level explanations part of the predictive computation. ChildRiskGuard achieves an F1 score of 0.769, substantially outperforming direct application of a general-purpose content-safety model while remaining competitive with strong benchmarks. The primary contribution is AI4CDS as a responsible framework for AI-enabled CDS; ChildRiskGuard provides process and artifact evidence of how AI-expanded, researcher-governed design can generate and evaluate novel computational design knowledge.

</details>


### [241] [Don't Drop Dropout: Optimizing Layer Sparsity for Efficient LLM Training and Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05275)
*Mostafa Elhoushi,Alex Pretko,Nolan Dey,Bin Claire Zhang,Gavia Gray,Gurpreet Gosal,Abdulrahman Mahmoud,Shane Bergsma,Joel Hestness*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了层丢弃（layer dropout）在大规模语言模型预训练中的价值，通过超过2400次实验（模型271M至8.2B参数，数据最高160B tokens），证明了在最优层分布、时间调度和优化器超参数下，层丢弃可以在相同训练FLOPs下降低损失、节省最高25%训练FLOPs，并支持早期退出、层跳跃和自推测解码等后训练优化，实现高达1.5倍推理加速且精度损失可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层丢弃（随机深度）已被证明在语言和视觉Transformer中能加速训练、提升精度并增强对零样本层剪枝的鲁棒性。然而随着模型和数据集规模扩大，层丢弃在LLM预训练中几乎被弃用。尽管有先前工作报告dropout会降低精度，但缺乏全面研究来量化甚至缓解这一影响，因此有必要系统性探究层丢弃在现代LLM训练中的价值及其最优实践。

Method: 作者提出了一套针对LLM训练中层丢弃的最优实践方案，包括：(1) 最优层分布；(2) 时间调度（time schedule）；(3) 优化器超参数调优。在此基础上，通过超过2400次预训练实验系统评估层丢弃效果，涵盖模型规模271M至8.2B参数、数据集规模最高160B tokens，所有实验在Cerebras CS-3系统上完成。

Result: (1) 在相同训练FLOPs下，采用最优层丢弃的LLM可获得更低的验证损失；(2) 在给定训练步数下可节省高达25%的训练FLOPs而不牺牲性能；(3) 层丢弃支持的后期优化（早期退出、中间层跳跃、自推测解码）可实现高达1.5倍推理加速，且精度损失可忽略；(4) 上述结论在271M至8.2B参数模型及160B token数据规模上均保持一致，证明其可扩展性。

Conclusion: 层丢弃应当被纳入最先进的LLM训练流程中。通过合理的层分布调度、时间调度和优化器超参数设置，层丢弃不仅能在预训练阶段带来效率收益（节省高达25% FLOPs），还能为推理阶段提供显著加速（最高1.5倍），其收益在大型训练规模下可靠成立。

Abstract: Layer dropout (a.k.a. stochastic depth) has been shown to enable faster training, higher accuracy, and robustness to zero-shot layer pruning in both language and vision transformers. However, as models and datasets have scaled, dropout - particularly layer dropout - has largely disappeared from large language models (LLMs) pre-training recipes. While some prior work has reported that dropout can degrade accuracy, no comprehensive study has quantified, let alone mitigated, this effect. In this study, we show that layer dropout should be used in state-of-the-art LLM training, establishing best practices and scaling analysis for both training and post-training benefits. Concretely, with optimal layer distribution, time schedule, and optimizer hyperparameters, we observe that at the same training FLOPs layer dropout leads to lower loss. For a given number of training steps, LLMs can achieve lower or similar validation loss while saving upto 25% of training FLOPs. Moreover, layer dropout enables significant post-training optimizations, such as early exit, intermediate-layer skipping, and self-speculative decoding, yielding up to 1.5x inference speedup with negligible accuracy loss. Across more than 2400 training experiments, spanning models from 271M to 8.2B parameters and datasets up to 160B tokens, we demonstrate that these findings extend reliably to large-scale training regimes. All pre-training experiments were run on Cerebras CS-3 systems.

</details>


### [242] [Testing Interchangeability in LLM Agent Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05279)
*Jianxin Gao,Tianyi Yu,Linna Deng,Runze Li,Zining Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究检验了生产级多智能体系统中"智能体角色可互换"的假设。在多个环境中，研究者从同一基础模型独立组建团队并记录私有笔记，随后交换角色匹配的智能体，发现换员对任务得分影响小，但显著增加了团队沟通成本（每单位进展的沟通量提升16%-63%），且换员带来的干扰与团队历史漂移程度正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的多智能体系统经常替换智能体，默认假设同一角色的智能体可以互换。然而该假设从未经过严格的实证检验。理解换员对团队协调的隐形代价，对于部署和维护多智能体系统具有重要的实际意义。

Method: 从同一基础模型独立组建八支团队执行相同任务，每名智能体维护跨十个形成episode的私人笔记；然后对团队之间进行角色匹配的智能体交换，并在保留任务上测量影响。使用安慰剂（placebo）对照以复现阵容变更带来的干扰但不真正更换人员；在多种设置下进行三次消融实验，分别调整基础模型、解码温度和形成长度。

Result: 换员造成的任务得分损失很小，但通信效率显著下降（每单位进展的沟通量增加16%-63%）。在Hanabi中，被交换的智能体比缺乏经验的新手更"昂贵"，表明其与前任队友学到的惯例产生干扰。在Collab-Overcooked中，当负责设定议程的智能体被更换时，额外沟通主要来自留下的智能体。消融实验显示换员代价仅与团队独立形成的漂移程度这一指标共变：贪心解码降低两者，延长团队历史则同时提高两者。

Conclusion: 在这些设置中，智能体在任务结果上的可互换性高于在协调效率上的可互换性；形成历史越长的团队，换员的影响越大。这挑战了多智能体系统中智能体完全可互换的朴素假设，提示生产系统需要考虑换员带来的隐性协调成本。

Abstract: Production multi-agent systems replace agents constantly, on the assumption that an agent filling a role is interchangeable with any other agent that can do the job. We test that assumption. Eight teams per setting are formed independently from one base model on the same tasks, each agent keeping a private notebook across ten formation episodes; we then trade role-matched agents between teams and measure what changes on held-out tasks. Against a placebo that reproduces the disruption of a roster change without changing who occupies the seat, a swap costs little in task score but raises the communication a team spends per unit of progress by 16 to 63 percent, and in Hanabi a swapped agent is more expensive than an inexperienced one, consistent with interference from conventions learned with its former partner. In Collab-Overcooked, when the agent that sets the agenda is replaced, most of the extra communication comes from the agent that stayed. Three ablations, over base models, decoding temperature and formation length, move the swap penalty alongside one other quantity: how far independently formed teams drift apart. Greedy decoding lowers both; doubling a team's history raises both. In these settings, agents are more fungible in task outcome than in coordination efficiency, with larger swap effects after longer formation histories.

</details>


### [243] [GUT: Quantifying and Optimizing the Reasoning Uncertainty of LLMs via Graph Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05284)
*Shuang Liang,Xin-Yu Hu,Xiang-Jun Ou,Shao-Qun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于图复杂度的不确定性方法（GUT），通过有向无环图全面覆盖LLM推理链中的潜在分支，并构建量化模块（GUT-Q）和优化模块（GUT-O），分别用于度量（逼近推理空间复杂度）和降低（以负不确定性作为强化学习奖励函数）LLM的推理不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在推理过程中即使面对相同提示，也会在每一步产生大量发散分支，部分分支呈现出不可靠甚至荒谬的推理链和结果，这种推理不确定性亟需量化和缓解。

Method: GUT的核心思想是用有向无环图（DAG）来表征每条推理链的潜在分支，确保所有潜在分支在图空间内得到全面覆盖。在此基础上构建两个模块：GUT-Q通过图复杂度近似推理空间复杂度来度量LLM的推理不确定性；GUT-O将负不确定性作为强化学习的奖励函数来实现不确定性优化。

Result: 在四个LLM和五个数据集上的实验结果验证了GUT方法的有效性。

Conclusion: GUT方法能够有效地量化和降低LLM的推理不确定性，通过图复杂度理论为LLM推理提供了一种系统性的不确定性管理框架。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed great advances in the reasoning ability of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, the reasoning processes of LLMs often exhibit uncertainty, where LLMs often produce a proliferation of divergent branches at each reasoning step even when fed the same prompting inputs, and certain branches exhibit evidently incredible, even nonsensical, reasoning chains and results. In this paper, we propose the Graph-complexity-based UncerTainty (GUT) method for investigating the reasoning uncertainty of LLMs. The key idea of GUT is to characterize the potential branches of each reasoning chain with a directed acyclic graph, thereby ensuring that all potential branches are comprehensively covered within the graph space. Building upon this recognition, we further build two modules of GUT, that is, a Quantification (GUT-Q) module and an Optimization (GUT-O) module, for quantifying and reducing the reasoning uncertainty of LLMs, respectively. GUT-Q measures LLM reasoning uncertainty by approximating the reasoning space complexity with graph complexity. GUT-O implements uncertainty optimization by treating negative uncertainty as the reward function in reinforcement learning. Experimental results conducted on four LLMs and five datasets validate the effectiveness of GUT.

</details>


### [244] [Beyond Aggregate Scores: Behavioral Correctness Assumptions for Assessing Reference-Based Automatic Evaluation Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05289)
*Maria Mahbub,Ashley Rice,Michael R. Munroe,Amidu Kamara,Amir Sadovnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于评估自动评估方法（evaluators）的补充框架——"行为正确性假设"（behavioral correctness assumptions），通过受控的响应变换来检验评估器在行为层面的表现，而非仅依赖与人类判断的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的元评估主要测量评估器与人类判断或基准标签的一致性，缺乏在受控条件下对评估器行为进行深入理解的视角，难以诊断评估器在特定条件下的行为缺陷。

Method: 作者定义了"正确性保持"（correctness-preserving）和"正确性改变"（correctness-altering）假设的分类体系，通过受控的响应变换（controlled response transformations）来操作化这些假设，并规定了预期的评分行为。随后对多种评估器（词法级、字符级、语义级、LLM基础及混合型）进行了系统分析。

Result: 实验发现所有评估器均不满足全部行为正确性假设；即使聚合性能相似的评估器，其行为特征也可能存在显著差异。不同评估范式展现出不同的行为权衡。

Conclusion: 行为正确性假设框架能够提供传统聚合元评估所掩盖的诊断性信息，为自动评估方法的深入理解与选择提供了新视角。

Abstract: Automated reference-based evaluation methods play a critical role in assessing natural language generation systems. Existing meta-evaluation primarily measures agreement with human judgments or benchmark labels, providing limited insight into evaluator behavior under controlled conditions. We introduce behavioral correctness assumptions, a complementary framework for evaluating reference-based automatic evaluation methods. We define a taxonomy of correctness-preserving and correctness-altering assumptions and operationalize them through controlled response transformations that specify expected scoring behaviors. We evaluate diverse lexical, character-level, semantic, LLM-based, and hybrid evaluators and analyze their assumption-level behavior, stability, sensitivity, repeat-run variability, configuration sensitivity, and reproducibility. Our experiments reveal distinct behavioral trade-offs across evaluation paradigms: no evaluator satisfies all proposed correctness assumptions, and evaluators with similar aggregate performance can exhibit substantially different behavioral profiles. These findings demonstrate that behavioral correctness assumptions provide diagnostic information obscured by conventional aggregate meta-evaluation.

</details>


### [245] [RISE: Recursive Improvement via Self-Extrapolating Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05295)
*Yang Li,Semih Yavuz,Shafiq Joty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RISE方法，通过从模型自身RLVR训练轨迹中构建合成教师（在参数空间或logit空间外推当前checkpoint与锚点checkpoint之间的位移），将稀疏的结果级奖励更新转化为密集的token级蒸馏目标，无需外部教师或特权条件，实现递归式的策略自我提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的on-policy distillation（OPD）为语言模型后训练提供密集的per-token监督，但其效果受限于教师质量：外部教师存在分布偏移问题，而带特权条件的自蒸馏又受限于模型上下文学习能力。作者希望克服这两种瓶颈，在不依赖外部模型或特权监督的条件下获得高质量的密集监督信号。

Method: RISE通过以下方式实现：(1) 从模型自身的RLVR训练轨迹中获取当前checkpoint与trailing anchor之间的参数/输出logit位移；(2) 外推该位移以构建合成教师，将稀疏的结果驱动的参数更新转化为密集的token级目标；(3) 将RLVR与OPD形成互补循环：结果奖励引导外推朝向正确推理，外推教师精炼token级决策；(4) 每轮迭代随学生提升刷新教师，使蒸馏成为递归改进机制而非一次性压缩步骤。

Result: 在数学推理、多领域STEM、代码生成和多轮agent任务上，RISE在全部设置下均优于仅RLVR训练和on-policy自蒸馏基线。

Conclusion: RISE证明了无需外部教师或特权条件即可通过递归自外推蒸馏实现持续的策略改进，将蒸馏从单向知识压缩转变为可递归提升的训练机制，为语言模型后训练提供了一种新的自我改进范式。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense, per-token supervision for language model post-training, but its effectiveness is bottlenecked by teacher quality: external teachers suffer from distribution mismatch, while self-distillation with privileged conditioning is limited by in-context learning capacity. We propose \textbf{RISE} (\textbf{R}ecursive \textbf{I}mprovement via \textbf{S}elf-\textbf{E}xtrapolating Policy Distillation), which constructs a synthetic teacher directly from the model's own RLVR training trajectory. By extrapolating the displacement between the current checkpoint and a trailing anchor---in parameter space or output logit space---RISE converts a sparse outcome-induced parameter update into a dense token-level target, without any external model or privileged conditioning. RISE combines RLVR and OPD in a complementary loop: outcome rewards ground the extrapolation toward correct reasoning, while the extrapolated teacher refines token-level decisions. Moreover, since the teacher is refreshed every iteration as the student improves, distillation becomes a recursive improvement mechanism rather than a one-shot compression step. Experiments spanning mathematical reasoning, multi-domain STEM, code generation, and multi-turn agentic tasks show that RISE outperforms RLVR-only training and on-policy self-distillation across all settings.

</details>


### [246] [LLM-Driven Algorithm Design for Quantum Circuit Synthesis based on Binary Decision Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05327)
*Yoonju Sim,Federico Berto,Chuanbo Hua,Jinkyoo Park,Changhyun Kwon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出QuantumEvo，一个利用LLM作为启发式生成器的进化框架，用于搜索QCC感知的BDD变量排序启发式。发现的启发式HGA-QE通过修改遗传算法中的sifting步骤来更好地对齐量子电路成本，在基准测试中达到70.9%的tie-or-win率和13.5%的严格最优率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BDD变量排序对可逆电路综合结果至关重要，现有排序启发式主要优化BDD大小，但BDD大小只是合成电路量子成本(QCC)的不完美替代指标。因此需要直接针对QCC进行变量排序优化的方法。

Method: QuantumEvo是一个进化框架：(1) 使用LLM作为启发式生成器，而非直接预测排序；(2) 从多个启发式家族初始化搜索空间；(3) 候选启发式通过标准BDD操作直接操控变量排序；(4) 使用下游QCC作为选择标准评估候选启发式。最终发现的HGA-QE修改了遗传算法内部的sifting步骤，使其与QCC更好地对齐。

Result: 在基准测试中，HGA-QE达到70.9%的tie-or-win率（对比每个函数的最佳基线），在13.5%的函数上严格最优。在两个来自不同数据源的基准套件上，HGA-QE展现出更明显的严格获胜优势，证明其具有跨数据集的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，通过LLM驱动的进化搜索来发现QCC感知的排序启发式是可行且有效的，比直接优化BDD大小能更好地降低合成电路的量子成本，且发现的启发式具有跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Quantum circuits are central to implementing quantum algorithms on quantum devices, where quantum gates must be reversible. Many quantum algorithms rely on Boolean functions, which must therefore be implemented reversibly within quantum circuits. Reversible circuit synthesis provides a way to translate such Boolean functions into reversible circuits. Binary decision diagrams (BDDs) offer a scalable approach to this task, but the resulting BDDs and circuits depend heavily on variable ordering. Existing ordering heuristics commonly minimize BDD size because it is closely tied to the circuit size. However, BDD size is an imperfect proxy for the quantum cost of the synthesized circuit (QCC). We propose \texttt{QuantumEvo}, an evolutionary framework that uses an LLM as a heuristic generator for QCC-aware BDD variable ordering. Instead of predicting orderings directly, \texttt{QuantumEvo} searches over ordering heuristics initialized from multiple heuristic families. Candidate heuristics directly manipulate variable orderings using standard BDD operations and are selected by downstream QCC. The discovered heuristic, HGA-QE, modifies the sifting step inside a genetic algorithm so that the procedure is better aligned with QCC. Across the benchmark set, HGA-QE achieves a 70.9\% tie-or-win rate against the per-function best baseline and is strictly best on 13.5\% of the functions. The results demonstrate broadly competitive QCC performance, with HGA-QE showing a clearer relative advantage in strict wins on the two benchmark suites drawn from sources different from the data used for heuristic discovery.

</details>


### [247] [Who Should Grade My Work? Student Perspectives on Transparent AI-Assisted Writing Assessment in Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05346)
*Rayed AlGhamdi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过质性教学探究，考察了沙特公立大学本科计算机专业学生在明确知晓评分与反馈由ChatGPT生成的情况下如何理解和回应AI介导的评价。研究发现学生认可AI反馈对表层修订有用，但始终将人工教师视为评分决策的适当权威，并将"反馈效用"与"评价权威"视为两种分析上分离的判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究主要考察GenAI生成反馈能否提升写作表现以及学生如何感知其有用性，但鲜有研究关注当学生被明确告知评分和反馈由AI而非教师生成时，他们如何解读此类评价。这一认知层面的空白值得探究。

Method: 在沙特一所公立大学的本科技术交流课程中开展质性教学探究。13名男性计算机专业本科生完成课堂手写写作任务，扫描稿交由ChatGPT依据与任务目标一致的量规提示评估。随后明确告知学生评分与反馈由ChatGPT生成，并邀请他们书面反思该评价。对反思内容进行归纳式主题分析。

Result: 归纳式主题分析识别出四个主题：反馈的感知有用性；对AI情境性与教学局限性的意识；有条件信任（区分反馈效用与评价权威）；以及对人类教师制度性与教学角色的反思。参与者在表层修订层面接受GenAI反馈，但始终将人类教师定位为评分决策的适当权威。

Conclusion: 研究表明，学生将"反馈效用"与"评价权威"视为分析上分离的两类判断，而非同一认可量表上的对立两端。这一区分对高等教育中AI辅助评估的设计与实施具有重要启示。

Abstract: The integration of GenAI tools into higher education assessment raises important questions about how students understand, interpret, and respond to AI-mediated evaluation. As instructors increasingly explore AI tools for providing feedback, prior research has examined whether GenAI-generated feedback improves writing performance and how students perceive its usefulness; comparatively little is known, however, about how students interpret such evaluation when they are explicitly informed that an AI system, rather than a human instructor, produced the feedback and the score. This study reports findings from a qualitative pedagogical inquiry conducted in an undergraduate technical communication course for computing students at a Saudi public university. Thirteen male undergraduate computing students completed an in-class handwritten writing task; the scanned submissions were evaluated by ChatGPT using a rubric-based prompt aligned with the task objectives. Students were then explicitly informed that ChatGPT had generated the score and feedback and were invited to reflect on the evaluation in writing. Inductive thematic analysis of these reflections identified four themes: perceived usefulness of feedback; awareness of AI's contextual and pedagogical limitations; conditional trust, distinguishing feedback utility from evaluative authority; and reflection on the institutional and pedagogical role of the human instructor. Participants accepted GenAI feedback as useful for surface-level revision but consistently positioned the human instructor as the appropriate authority over grading decisions. The study identifies this as a distinction between feedback utility and evaluative authority, two judgments that students treat as analytically separate rather than as opposite ends of a single approval scale...

</details>


### [248] [CUA-Universe: A Scalable and Dynamic Environment for Hybrid GUI+CLI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05374)
*Haoting Shi,Wenhao Wang,Weicheng Fang,Yaozhong Liang,Tian Jin,Pengxiang Zhao,Guangyi Liu,Siheng Chen,Yanfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CUA-Universe，一个将真实桌面软件转化为混合GUI+Cli环境的可扩展数据管道，包含App-Forge（适配16个应用）、Task-Weave（合成可控难度的混合任务）和Path-Steer（引导高效混合轨迹）。在9B模型上，该方法在CUA-Verse、OSWorld和OSWorld-MCP上均显著提升了成功率并大幅减少了步骤和token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用智能体主要依赖GUI操作，产生低效轨迹；而真实计算机工作是混合式的，需要同时协调GUI视觉检查与CLI精确高效操作。然而，支持GUI和CLI双界面的可扩展混合环境极为稀缺（每个应用需大量手动工程），且现有智能体无法互补使用两种界面：CLI原生智能体缺乏视觉感知，GUI原生智能体对适用于命令的操作低效。

Method: 提出CUA-Universe可扩展环境到数据的管道，由三个组件构成：(1) App-Forge——将真实应用适配为可复现虚拟机并发现、包装或生成命令行界面，扩展至16个应用；(2) Task-Weave——从种子文件的可复用操作中合成难度可控的多样化混合任务；(3) Path-Steer——沿高效混合路径引导rollout并收获已验证轨迹用于后训练。

Result: 9B模型在三个方面表现优异：CUA-Verse上Score提升+39.3分，步骤减少37%，token减少60%；OSWorld上SR提升+16.8分，步骤减少57%，token减少44%；OSWorld-MCP上Score提升+7.84分，步骤减少27%，token减少30%。训练将智能体行为从低效的GUI交互和脆弱的CLI脚本转向有效的GUI+Cli编排。

Conclusion: CUA-Universe为更强大且高效的计算机使用智能体提供了可扩展的路径，验证了混合GUI+Cli训练数据能够同时改善智能体的成功率和操作效率，代表了GUI操作和CLI脚本之间的有效桥梁。

Abstract: Computer-use agents have advanced on benchmarks like OSWorld and AndroidWorld, but still act mostly through the GUI, often producing inefficient trajectories. Real-world computer work is hybrid, combining visual-state inspection with precise, high-throughput command-line operations, so capable agents must coordinate both modalities over shared application state. Yet scalable hybrid environments remain scarce because supporting both GUI and CLI over real applications typically requires substantial manual engineering for each application. Existing agents also struggle to use the two interfaces complementarily: CLI-native agents lack visual perception for tasks involving interface state or layout, while GUI-native agents are inefficient for operations better executed through commands. We introduce CUA-Universe, a scalable environment-to-data pipeline that turns real desktop software into hybrid GUI+CLI environments. App-Forge adapts applications into reproducible VMs and command-line surfaces it discovers, wraps, or generates, scaling to 16 applications; Task-Weave synthesizes diverse hybrid tasks of controllable difficulty from reusable operations over seed files; and Path-Steer steers rollouts along efficient hybrid paths and harvests verified trajectories for post-training. Training on this data shifts behavior from inefficient GUI interaction and brittle CLI scripting toward effective GUI+CLI orchestration. Our 9B model improves both success and efficiency on CUA-Verse (Score +39.3 pts; -37% steps, -60% tokens), OSWorld (SR +16.8 pts; -57% steps, -44% tokens), and OSWorld-MCP (Score +7.84 pts; -27% steps, -30% tokens). CUA-Universe provides a scalable path toward more capable and efficient computer-use agents.

</details>


### [249] [Molecular Déjà Vu: Digit-Level Retrieval of Published Values in Frontier Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05381)
*Matthias Busch,Marius Tacke,Sviatlana V. Lamaka,Mikhail L. Zheludkevich,Christian J. Cyron,Roland C. Aydin,Christian Feiler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了22个前沿大语言模型在12个分子回归基准上的逐字检索（verbatim retrieval）现象，发现该现象普遍存在但具有基准特异性，推理水平会影响检索概率，并测试了干扰检索的方法，揭示了各模型预测能力差异并非仅由记忆量决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在分子性质基准上的准确率无法区分模型是真正预测了性质还是从训练数据中检索了已发表的数值，这导致对LLM科学推理能力的评估可能被污染，需要系统性地审计和量化这种逐字检索现象。

Method: 对22个前沿LLM在12个回归基准上进行审计，通过设计两种推理水平（低/高）的实验比较检索差异，并针对污染最严重的案例设计了变换SMILES字符串的干扰实验来中断检索，分析预测误差的分布变化。

Result: 逐字检索广泛存在但具有基准特异性：5个数据集上超过50%的LLM表现出逐字检索；高推理水平下被标记的案例比低推理水平多89%；干扰检索后最强模型在某些情况下仍能识别变换后的SMILES与原始标签的组合；抑制检索使各模型预测误差相对更加接近。

Conclusion: LLM的预测能力不仅由记忆值的数量决定，逐字检索在分子回归基准中广泛存在但深度因模型和基准而异，推理水平显著影响检索行为，需要开发更鲁棒的评估方案以区分真实预测与记忆检索。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly evaluated on molecular property benchmarks, but accuracy cannot distinguish a model that predicts a property from one that retrieves a published number. We audit 22 frontier models on 12 regression benchmarks for verbatim retrieval and find that it is widespread but relatively benchmark-specific: on five datasets more than $50\%$ of the LLMs show verbatim retrieval, while on the remaining datasets it appears only in isolated cells. We run our experiments at two reasoning levels and find that reasoning changes retrieval. The same experiments, on the same molecules and with the same prompt, are flagged $89\%$ more often at the higher reasoning level than at the lowest one. Finally, we test a way to interrupt retrieval in our most contaminated cases, and find that the strongest models in some cases still recognise a combination of transformed SMILES strings and original labels. Furthermore, suppressing retrieval moves the prediction errors of the different models closer together in relative terms, while their differing use of verbatim retrieval spreads them apart. This indicates that the general predictive capability of an LLM is not determined solely by the amount of memorised values. This work provides an overview of the amount and depth of verbatim retrieval in molecular regression benchmarks using LLMs.

</details>


### [250] [Necessary or Sufficient? Evaluating LLM Explanations With Behavioural Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05385)
*Urja Pawar,Rajitha Ramanayake,Nabeel Kemal,Ashwin Kandath,Owen O'Neill,Guillaume Bourgeon,Houssem Chatbri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM决策组件在智能体工作流中生成的因素解释（named factors）是否与其可观测的决策行为一致。作者提出了两种解释解释：必要性（改变因素会改变输出）和充分性（保留因素而消除其他可变化信息可保持输出）。通过在两个合成用例（顾问推荐和提示词危害判断）中，对Claude、GPT、Gemini家族共8个模型进行黑盒干预测试，发现模型引用的因素排名与必要性/充分性得分之间的平均Spearman相关性仅为0.349-0.580，且未被引用的因素在大量情况下（高达58.1%）得分高于最低引用的因素。结论是：引用的因素包含有用信息，但并不能可靠地识别实际影响最强的三个因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能体工作流中，运营者依赖LLM提供的解释（即命名的关键因素）来监控系统、诊断错误或决定何时升级输出。这隐含假设解释与组件的可观测决策行为一致。然而，该假设尚未被系统验证。本文旨在检验LLM解释中命名的因素是否符合其实际决策行为，以评估解释在智能体监督中的可靠性。

Method: 本文提出了两种解释解释的操作化定义：（1）必要性——改变该因素应当改变输出；（2）充分性——保留该因素同时去除其他可变化信息应保持输出不变。采用受控黑盒干预方法估计每个因素的necessity score（改变该因素改变输出的频率）和sufficiency score（保留该因素保持输出的频率）。在两种合成用例（向客户推荐顾问、审查提示词的有害性/风险）中对来自Claude、GPT、Gemini三个模型家族的8个模型进行评估。模型输出决策及影响最大的三个因素的排名，与干预测量的得分进行比较。使用Spearman相关分析引用的排名与实际影响得分的一致性，并统计未被引用因素得分超过最低引用因素的比例。

Result: 在8个模型中：（1）引用的因素排名与necessity和sufficiency得分的平均Spearman相关系数分别为0.349和0.354（顾问推荐任务），以及0.431和0.580（提示词监控任务）。（2）未被引用的因素在57.6%（necessity）和58.1%（sufficiency）的顾问推荐响应中得分高于最低引用的因素；对应提示词监控的比例为25.8%和8.9%。（3）结论是引用的前三个因素确实包含有用信息，但并不能可靠地识别在必要性或充分性解释下实际影响最强的三个因素。

Conclusion: 论文提供了一个黑盒可靠性检查框架，可用于智能体监督中评估LLM解释是否与其决策行为一致。研究发现模型引用的解释因素与实际行为影响的匹配度有限，建议运营者不应将此类解释视为对其决策过程的准确描述。该框架仅适用于单个LLM决策的范围，不扩展到多步骤智能体行为。此工作为检测和校准LLM解释的可靠性提供了实证基础和方法工具。

Abstract: LLM decision components that can operate within agent workflows often produce action-relevant recommendations or judgements together with explanations. Operators may use the named factors to monitor a system, diagnose errors, or decide when to escalate an output. Such use assumes that the explanations agree with the component's observable decision behaviour. We test two interpretations of the named factors: necessity, meaning that changing a factor would change the output, and sufficiency, meaning that retaining it while removing other changeable information would preserve the output. We evaluate these interpretations in two synthetic use cases: recommending advisors to clients and judging prompts for harmfulness or risk. Models return an output and the top three factors that most influenced it. Controlled black-box interventions estimate a necessity score for each factor by measuring how often changing it changes the output, and a sufficiency score by measuring how often retaining it preserves the output. Across eight models from the Claude, GPT, and Gemini families, the mean Spearman correlations between the cited ranking and the necessity and sufficiency scores are 0.349 and 0.354 for advisor recommendation, and 0.431 and 0.580 for prompt monitoring. Furthermore, an uncited factor scores above the lowest-scoring cited factor in 57.6% of advisor responses under necessity and 58.1% under sufficiency; the corresponding prompt-monitoring rates are 25.8% and 8.9%. The cited top three contain useful information but do not reliably identify the three factors with the strongest measured influence under necessity or sufficiency. The framework provides a black-box reliability check for explanations used in agent oversight while remaining scoped to individual LLM decisions.

</details>


### [251] [A Deep Generative Model for Synthesizing Labeled Wireless Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05396)
*Yuxiao Li,Keke Hu,Santiago Mazuelas,Yuan Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度学习的无线信号生成方法IIns-GAN（Inter-Instance Generative Adversarial Networks），用于生成逼真的带标签无线信号，以克服真实世界数据集采集成本和标注成本高昂的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线感知需要带位置标签的无线信号用于模型训练和性能评估，但真实数据集的获取面临高测量和标注成本，而传统合成方法依赖环境模型，需要大量超参数调优且生成信号的逼真度不足以支撑全面的模型训练。

Method: 提出Inter-Instance Generative Adversarial Networks (IIns-GAN)，一种基于生成对抗网络的深度学习方法来生成逼真的带标签无线信号，能够适应不同环境场景。

Result: 在公开的超宽带（UWB）数据集上进行了大量实验，结果表明IIns-GAN生成的信号能反映真实测量的物理特性，并显著提升多种无线感知任务（如距离估计和环境识别）的模型训练效果。

Conclusion: IIns-GAN能够生成物理特性逼真且环境自适应的带标签无线信号，有效缓解真实数据采集成本高的瓶颈问题，对多种无线感知任务中模型训练的改进具有显著贡献。

Abstract: Wireless signals with position-related labels are pivotal for both performance evaluation and model training in the realm of wireless sensing. However, acquiring real-world datasets is often challenged by significant measurement and labeling costs. Traditional methods for synthesizing labeled wireless signals typically rely on environmental models, leading to extensive hyper-parameter tuning and inadequate realism for comprehensive model training purposes. To address these limitations, we introduce a novel deep learning (DL)-based method, namely Inter-Instance Generative Adversarial Networks (IIns-GAN), to generate realistic labeled wireless signals. The generated signals are particularly adaptive to different environment scenarios and well-suited for various model training tasks, including distance estimation and environment identification. We have conducted extensive experiments on public Ultra-Wideband (UWB) datasets to evaluate the realism and utility of the generated signals. The results demonstrate that the signals generated by IIns-GAN mirror the physical characteristics of real-world measurements, and significantly contribute to the improvement of model training in diverse wireless sensing tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [252] [FailureSpot: Label-Efficient Timestamp-Level Failure Detection for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04277)
*Jie Ma,Zongxi Liu,Yi Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种数据高效的VLA失败检测框架，利用未标注的动作块构建弱监督信号，并结合主动学习选择最具不确定性的轨迹进行时间戳级标注，从而提升细粒度失败检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略在长时间执行中会不可预测地失败，可靠的失败检测对安全部署至关重要。现有方法要么依赖视觉模型在错误动作发生后才能检测，要么使用基于VLA内部表示的轻量级主动检测器，但这些主动方法常用轨迹级标签进行监督，导致不成功轨迹中正常的失败前行为被错误标记为失败，产生标签噪声，限制了轨迹级检测精度和时间戳级故障定位。

Method: 提出数据高效框架：(1) 利用未标记的VLA动作块构建动作派生的弱监督信号，捕捉不一致的连续动作块、冻结或空闲动作、激进随机运动等异常模式；(2) 使用主动学习仅选择最不确定的轨迹进行时间戳级标注；(3) 用这些信息丰富的标签微调检测器。

Result: 在多个VLA策略上的实验表明，该方法同时提升了时间戳级和轨迹级失败检测性能。

Conclusion: 通过弱监督信号与主动学习的结合，该方法以数据高效的方式解决了轨迹级标签噪声问题，实现了精确的时间戳级VLA失败检测，对安全可靠的机器人操控具有重要意义。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies have shown strong potential for general-purpose robotic manipulation, but they can still fail unpredictably during long-horizon execution, making reliable failure detection essential for safe deployment. Existing methods either rely on visual models that typically detect failures only after erroneous actions have occurred, or use lightweight proactive detectors trained on VLA internal representations. However, these proactive methods are often supervised with trajectory-level labels, causing normal pre-failure behavior in unsuccessful trajectories to be incorrectly labeled as failure. This supervision mismatch introduces label noise and limits both trajectory-level detection accuracy and precise timestamp-level failure localization. In this work, we study fine-grained timestamp-level VLA failure detection while addressing the cost of dense annotation. We propose a data-efficient framework that first leverages unlabeled VLA action chunks to construct action-derived weak supervision signals, capturing abnormal patterns such as inconsistent consecutive chunks, frozen or idle actions, and aggressive random motions. We then use active learning to select only the most uncertain trajectories for timestamp-level annotation and fine-tune the detector with these informative labels. Experiments across multiple VLA policies show that our method improves both timestamp-level and trajectory-level failure detection performance.

</details>


### [253] [VLA-Precision: Asymmetric Co-Bootstrapping for Efficient Real-World Online RL of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04355)
*Chenyu Su,Zhaolong Shen,Yuan Qian,Chen Qian,Rui Zhang,Feng Yan,Weixing Chen,Fei Zhang,Jiamin Wang,Shuang Cong,Weiwei Shang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-Precision是一个面向高精度机器人操作任务的真实世界在线强化学习框架，通过非对称共自举（ACoB）算法和ACoB-Stream架构，在9个高精度化学任务和4种机器人平台上实现98.3%的平均成功率，每任务仅需45.8分钟训练，吞吐量提升最高10.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在广泛操作任务上表现良好，但在需要高精度和可重复性的任务中仍不够可靠。真实世界在线强化学习（RL）用于VLA后训练可实现自主试错改进，但存在两个瓶颈：1）不可靠的价值信号会导致策略漂移；2）大型VLA的计算开销限制了吞吐量和样本效率。作者旨在解决这两个瓶颈以实现高效精确的VLA后训练。

Method: 提出VLA-Precision框架，包含两个核心组件：1）ACoB（Asymmetric Co-Bootstrapping）算法，在不同时间尺度上建立非对称共自举：早期通过干预引导的行为学习快速提升策略性能并改善在线经验质量；随着自主经验积累，全局回报传播和局部偏好排序逐步校准价值估计，产生相对动作优势用于参考正则化的策略改进，同时抑制策略漂移。2）ACoB-Stream架构，一种闭环经验-策略架构，建立不变状态解耦和按需流式传输的设计原则，以适应大型VLA的高效训练。

Result: 在四个类别的九个高精度化学任务和四种机器人平台上评估，VLA-Precision实现98.3%的平均成功率，平均每任务仅需45.8分钟；单次训练时长27.6秒，运行速度分别为VLA基线和RL基线的1.2倍和1.8倍；吞吐量和计算效率最高提升10.9倍。

Conclusion: VLA-Precision通过ACoB算法解决价值信号不可靠导致的策略漂移问题，通过ACoB-Stream架构解决大型VLA的吞吐量瓶颈，在高精度化学任务中实现了高效率、高成功率的真实世界在线RL的VLA后训练，验证了其方法的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) models enable broad manipulation but remain unreliable in tasks demanding precision and repeatability. Applying real-world online reinforcement learning (RL) to VLA post-training enables autonomous trial-and-error improvement beyond demonstrations alone, but exposes two bottlenecks: 1) unreliable value signals can induce policy drift; 2) large-VLA overhead constrains throughput and sample efficiency. To address these challenges, we present VLA-Precision, an efficient real-world online RL framework featuring the Asymmetric Co-Bootstrapping (ACoB) algorithm and the ACoB-Stream architecture. Specifically, ACoB establishes asymmetric co-bootstrapping across timescales: early intervention-guided behavioral learning rapidly improves policy performance while enhancing online experience quality. As autonomous experience accumulates, global return propagation and local preference ranking progressively calibrate value estimates, yielding relative action advantages for reference-regularized policy improvement while suppressing drift. To enable ACoB on large VLAs, we develop ACoB-Stream, a closed-loop experience--policy architecture that establishes invariant-state decoupling and on-demand streaming as design principles, delivering up to 10.9$\times$ improvements in throughput and computational efficiency. Extensive evaluations on nine high-precision chemistry tasks across four categories and four robot embodiments show that VLA-Precision achieves 98.3\% mean success rate in 45.8 min/task, with 27.6 s episodes running at 1.2$\times$ and 1.8$\times$ the speeds of VLA and RL baselines. Resources are available at https://vla-precision.github.io.

</details>


### [254] [Scalable Edge-assisted Fusion and Path Prediction for Connected Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04364)
*Tyler Landle,Jackson Isenberg,Abhijit Chatterjee,Alexandros Daglis,Umakishore Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Conductor是一个基于边缘计算的车联网解决方案，从固定锚点（如RSU）视角创建统一世界模型并预测自动驾驶车辆的未来轨迹，通过遮挡感知选择器和运行时控制器联合工作，在严格的AoI时间预算内动态限制参与融合的车辆数量以保证服务质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆的规划算法受限于车载传感器的视线遮挡。边缘辅助的统一世界模型和轨迹预测能提升规划质量，但信息必须在紧的AoI时间预算内到达。现有SOTA采用逐CAV融合方式，限制了可扩展性和结果质量。

Method: Conductor提出两组件联合方案：（1）遮挡感知选择器，优先选择能检测到RSU未覆盖区域的AV进行信息贡献；（2）运行时控制器，动态调整每周期融合的AV数量与轨迹预测量以适应AoI时间预算。

Result: 在CAV仿真平台上，Conductor在最多31个CAV的各种交通场景下均满足AoI安全界限，融合保真度接近Oracle基准，远优于相同AoI约束下的随机选择器。

Conclusion: 联合选择器-控制器机制能够在AoI约束下有效平衡融合质量与延迟，证明了边缘端统一世界模型方案的可行性与优越性，为CAV系统的大规模增强提供了有效路径。

Abstract: The planning algorithms inside an Autonomous Vehicle (AV) rely on information from on-board sensors whose line of sight is limited by emerging traffic conditions and occlusions. Edge-assisted creation of a unified world model fusing information from AVs and Road Side Units (RSUs) in a geographical locale, and the prediction of AVs' future trajectories, can enhance the
  planning algorithms inside AVs to improve quality metrics, such as better traffic flow and collision prevention. AVs participating in such enhancements are called Connected Autonomous Vehicles (CAVs). However, such information generated by the edge (world model and motion predictions) must reach the planners within a tight Age of Information (AoI) time budget to be
  useful. The state of the art fuses per-CAV information: each AV fuses inputs from other actors locally, which limits both scalability with actor count and quality of results.
  We present Conductor, an edge-based solution for creating a unified world model from the perspective of a fixed anchor (e.g., an RSU) in a locale and predicting future trajectories of AVs in that locale. Our solution adheres to the AoI time budget by dynamically limiting the number of AVs that would lead to the best quality of results. Specifically, we introduce an
  occlusion-aware selector that favors information contribution by AVs that detect objects in the locale not covered by RSUs. We pair this selector with a runtime controller that adapts both the number of AV inputs to fuse and the amount of trajectory predictions in each cycle to stay within the AoI time budget. Evaluation on CAV simulation infrastructure shows our joint
  selector-controller meets the AoI safety bound across traffic scenarios with up to 31 CAVs, with fusion fidelity close to an Oracle and much better than a random selector under the same AoI constraint.

</details>


### [255] [AquaBEV: Monocular Underwater BEV Occupancy with 3D Sonar Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04411)
*Trung Tien Dong,Shengji Jin,Chen Chen,Yi Sheng,Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AquaBEV是一个单目水下占用预测模型，从单张RGB图像预测局部鸟瞰视角（BEV）占用情况，并在训练期间使用配对的3D成像声纳作为几何监督。模型将视觉特征映射至免校准的极坐标表示，沿距离维度进行因果解码，再重建为笛卡尔BEV坐标。在统一协议下建立的受控水下占用基准中，AquaBEV以31.4 Visible IoU和38.6 Observed IoU分别超越最强迁移基线4.0%和4.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主水下机器人在勘探、监测和巡检中需要安全的导航能力，而这依赖于理解周围自由与被占据空间。虽然BEV占用提供了这种表示，但从单张水下RGB图像预测它极其困难，因为仅凭外观难以获取可靠且充分的几何线索。3D成像声纳能提供互补的几何测量作为监督信号，但此前尚无将两者结合的单目水下占用预测方法。

Method: AquaBEV采用编码器将单张RGB图像提取视觉特征，通过免校准的极坐标表示映射特征（避免依赖相机标定），沿距离维度执行因果解码以利用声纳测距的物理特性，最终在笛卡尔BEV坐标系中重建占用预测。训练阶段使用与RGB图像配对的3D成像声纳提供几何监督。为了公平评估，建立了受控水下占用基准，将代表性占用方法统一适配到RGB到声纳任务上进行比较。

Result: AquaBEV在受控水下占用基准上取得31.4 Visible IoU和38.6 Observed IoU，较最强的迁移基线分别相对提升4.0%和4.3%。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了利用3D成像声纳作为训练期几何监督来引导单目水下RGB占用预测的有效性，所提出的免校准极坐标表示与沿距离范围的因果解码策略优于现有方法的跨域迁移表现，为水下机器人基于视觉的安全导航提供了新的可行方案。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater robots are widely used for exploration, monitoring, and inspection, where safe navigation depends on understanding the surrounding free and occupied space. Bird's eye view (BEV) occupancy provides such a representation, but predicting it from a single underwater RGB image is difficult due to limited, unreliable geometric cues from appearance alone. 3D imaging sonar offers complementary geometric measurements to supervise this task.
  We introduce AquaBEV, a monocular underwater occupancy model that predicts local BEV occupancy from a single RGB image, using paired 3D imaging sonar as geometric supervision during training. AquaBEV maps visual features into a calibration free polar representation and applies causal decoding along the range dimension before reconstructing the prediction in Cartesian BEV coordinates. A controlled underwater occupancy benchmark was established, adapting representative occupancy methods to the same RGB to sonar task under a unified protocol. AquaBEV achieves 31.4 Visible IoU and 38.6 Observed IoU, 4.0% and 4.3% relative improvements over the strongest transferred baseline.

</details>


### [256] [Achieving Asymptotic Near-Optimality Without $δ$-Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04464)
*Michael Moncton,Eric Frew*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文揭示了基于采样的运动规划算法中，渐近δ相似性证明依赖一个未明说的假设（即采样到的δ相似轨迹段会被保留），但该假设在"挤出效应"（crowding out）下不成立；文章证明在正确处理挤出效应后，即使没有δ相似解轨迹的保证，渐近近最优性仍可实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多基于采样的运动规划算法声称通过证明状态空间中与最优轨迹接近的δ相似轨迹的几乎必然采样来实现渐近近最优性。本文发现这一证明背后存在一个未言明的假设，该假设在一般情况下不成立，可能导致理论保证与实际行为之间的鸿沟。

Method: 本文首先识别并形式化描述了"挤出效应"问题——即局部低代价路径会阻止与最优轨迹δ相似的轨迹被添加到搜索树中；随后证明即使在无δ相似解轨迹保证的情况下，只要正确考虑挤出效应，渐近近最优性仍可达成；最后通过具体环境和系统实例展示了挤出效应确实会发生，并说明此时归纳采样δ相似解轨迹是不可能的。

Result: 证明了原有δ相似性证明中隐含假设的不成立性，并展示了挤出效应发生的具体环境与系统实例，同时给出了在考虑挤出效应后仍可保持渐近近最优性保证的结论。

Conclusion: 基于采样的运动规划算法的渐近近最优性可以通过正确考虑挤出效应来保证，而不必依赖δ相似解轨迹的存在性；这为算法的理论分析提供了更坚实的框架。

Abstract: Sampling-based motion planning algorithms are a popular class of trajectory planning algorithm due to their speed in complex, high-dimensional environments and ability to handle kinodynamic constraints, specifically through the use of forward dynamics propagation. Many such planners claim to achieve asymptotic near-optimality by proving the almost sure sampling of trajectories that are close to an optimal trajectory in the state space, known as $δ$-similar trajectories. This paper shows that the proof behind asymptotic $δ$-similarity relies on an unstated assumption that $δ$-similar trajectory segments will always be kept once sampled. This assumption does not hold in general. A problematic case, referred to as ``crowding out,'' is described, where locally low-cost paths prevent trajectories that are $δ$-similar to the optimal trajectory from being added to the tree. It is shown, however, that asymptotic near-optimality guarantees can still be achieved without guarantees of $δ$-similar solution trajectories when crowding out is properly accounted for. An example environment and system are provided where crowding out is shown to occur, demonstrating a scenario where inductively sampling a $δ$-similar solution trajectory is impossible.

</details>


### [257] [SocioGesture: Real-Time and Adaptive Social Gesture Perception for Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04545)
*Wenjin Fu,Li-Fan Wu,Jerin Peter,Chip Huyen,Boyuan Chen,Jan Liphardt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SocioGesture是一个用于人机交互的实时自适应社交手势感知系统，采用置信度感知的躯干-手部骨骼表示和轻量级双流模型实现低延迟识别，并通过遮挡感知的骨骼损坏训练增强部署鲁棒性，在机器人边缘设备上实现实时运行，同时支持通过离线标注扩展手势词汇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在真实环境中与人类交互时，不仅需要识别显式命令，还需理解社交线索（如邀请、拒绝、不可用等）。然而实际部署中，这些线索必须在部分遮挡、视角变化和严格延迟约束下的嘈杂机载感知中推断，现有系统在此类条件下鲁棒性和实时性不足。

Method: 1) 提出紧致的置信度感知身体-手部骨骼表示；2) 设计融合身体运动与手部关节的轻量级双流模型，支持低延迟机载识别；3) 采用遮挡感知的骨骼损坏训练策略（缺失手部、遮挡手臂、时间不稳定关键点）提升鲁棒性且不增加推理成本；4) 部署时保存不确定交互片段用于离线标注与适应，在不损失原类别性能的前提下扩展手势词汇。

Result: 在混合室内外HRI场景的社交手势数据集上，SocioGesture实现了强主体外泛化（held-out-subject）识别性能，在结构化关节遮挡下显著提升鲁棒性，并能在机器人机载边缘设备上实时运行。

Conclusion: SocioGesture展示了构建鲁棒、高效且自适应的社交感知系统用于交互式机器人的实用路径，为真实部署环境下的社交手势识别提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Robots interacting with people must recognize not only explicit commands, but also social cues such as invitations, refusals, and unavailability. In real deployments, these cues must be inferred from noisy onboard perception under partial occlusion, changing viewpoints, and strict latency constraints. We present SocioGesture, a real-time adaptive social gesture perception system for human-robot interaction (HRI). SocioGesture uses a compact confidence-aware body-hand skeleton representation and a lightweight dual-stream model that fuses body motion with hand articulation for low-latency onboard recognition. To improve deployment robustness, we train the model with occlusion-aware skeleton corruption, exposing it to missing hands, occluded arms, and temporally unstable keypoints without increasing the inference cost. On a social gesture dataset collected in mixed indoor-outdoor HRI scenarios, SocioGesture achieves strong held-out-subject recognition, substantially improves robustness under structured joint occlusion, and runs in real time on a robot-mounted edge device. During deployment, uncertain interaction segments are saved for offline labeling and adaptation, enabling SocioGesture to expand its gesture vocabulary while preserving performance in the original classes. These results demonstrate a practical path toward robust, efficient, and adaptive social perception for interactive robots.

</details>


### [258] [Continual Field-Adaptive Models (CFAMs) for Post-Deployment Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04552)
*Amarjot Singh,Tanmay R. Pancholi,Jainam Kothari,Shrirang Mahajan,Ketan Bansal,Zackory Erickson,Giuseppe Loianno,Alexandre M. Bayen,Jeff Schneider,Vince Nakayama*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出连续现场自适应模型（CFAMs），使机器人在训练后通过自主、无梯度、设备端更新继续学习。CFAM采用慢速学习组件（三皮层：传感器、推理、动作）与快速学习的胶囊场（Capsule Field）互补架构，实现实验室少样本技能安装和现场持续学习。在五种实体（机械臂、四足、人形、四旋翼、越野车）上验证，用40%数据达到全量数据训练性能，现场自主捕获近OOD案例使动作成功率提升13.9个百分点，在顺序模拟中反向迁移仅-0.5个百分点（对比LoRA的-11.4）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务关键操作中缺少无人值守的交互式自主能力——机器人在人类缺席时替代人类执行危险任务并使用人类工具。这些领域训练数据稀少且仅有板载计算资源，部署系统需面对新异情况且不能遗忘已有能力。

Method: CFAM采用互补学习架构：1)冻结的慢速学习组件包含传感器皮层（多模态输入映射为3D几何）、推理皮层（任务分解为技能并评估结果）、动作皮层（执行几何技能）；2)快速学习组件为胶囊场（Capsule Field），以一次性、无梯度方式存储胜任力胶囊（Competence Capsules）。实验室中少样本安装技能，现场持续学习；近OOD（开放世界新颖性不在范围内）数据经自主验证后自动学习。

Result: 在五种实体上对比pi0、CogACT、SpatialVLA等基线：CFAM用40%数据（2.5倍更少的轨迹）即达到全量预训练数据集上标准策略的性能水平；测试时自主捕获已验证的近OOD案例使动作成功率提升13.9个百分点；顺序模拟中反向迁移为-0.5个百分点，远优于LoRA的-11.4个百分点。

Conclusion: CFAM提供了有界形式的部署后物理智能：少样本技能学习、基于已验证近OOD经验的自主动态增长，以及保持先前能力，弥补了无人值守交互自主系统在任务关键场景中的缺失能力。

Abstract: Unattended interactive autonomy - machines that step into danger in place of humans and complete tasks with human tools - remains a missing capability in mission-critical operations. These domains offer scarce training data and only onboard compute, yet deployed systems must face novelty without erasing prior competence.
  We introduce Continual Field-Adaptive Models (CFAMs), which learn efficiently in the lab and continue learning after deployment through autonomous, gradient-free, on-device updates. CFAM uses a complementary learning architecture with a frozen slow-learning component and a fast-learning Capsule Field. The slow component contains three cortices: Sensor, which maps multimodal input into 3D-grounded geometry; Reasoning, which decomposes tasks into skills and evaluates outcomes; and Action, which executes geometric skills. The Capsule Field stores field learning one-shot and gradient-free as Competence Capsules. Skill installation is few-shot in the lab and continual in the field; open-world novelty is outside scope.
  We evaluate CFAM across five embodiments: manipulator, quadruped, humanoid, quadrotor, and off-road vehicle. Baselines (pi0, CogACT, SpatialVLA) use the same in-house multi-embodiment dataset for physical-platform comparisons. CFAM reaches the operating point of a standard policy trained on the full prior-training dataset using 40% of the data, or 2.5x fewer trajectories. At test time, autonomous capture of verified near-OOD cases improves action success by 13.9 percentage points. In sequential simulation, backward transfer is -0.5 percentage points versus -11.4 for LoRA. CFAM therefore provides a bounded form of post-deployment physical intelligence: few-shot skill learning, autonomous field growth from verified near-OOD experience, and retention of prior competence.

</details>


### [259] [NavArena: Automated Construction of Goal-Oriented Navigation Benchmarks from 3D Gaussian Splatting Reconstructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04602)
*Junhui Wang,Wei Yang,Xinyao Li,Ningjing Fan,Yuehao Yin,Xuecheng Chen,Chao Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavArena是一个自动化框架，将固定的3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）重建转化为面向目标导向视觉导航的基准测试平台。它集成冻结3DGS模型用于第一视角RGB-D渲染、基于高斯密度和高程统计的可达性/碰撞查询占用成本图，以及从多视角开放词汇掩码中提取的语义目标候选，从而实现导航episode的自动生成和统一闭环评估。在超过2000个场景中生成2220万个专家轨迹，验证了该框架在可扩展、可复现的3DGS导航评估方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定3DGS重建虽然能提供逼真的新视角渲染，但缺乏可穿越性约束、有效目标和评估导航所需的闭环协议，无法直接用于导航评估。现有导航评估要么依赖真实环境成本高昂，要么缺乏大规模、可复现的仿真场景。

Method: NavArena框架包含三个核心组件：(1)冻结的3DGS模型用于生成第一视角RGB-D渲染；(2)基于高斯密度和高程统计构建的占用成本图，用于可达性和碰撞查询；(3)从多视角开放词汇掩码中提升出的语义目标候选。这些组件共同支持目标导向导航episode的自动生成与统一闭环评估。

Result: 在超过2000个场景中，NavArena自动生成了2220万个专家轨迹。通过空间与语义评估检验了所构建的导航表示质量，并通过策略交互测试验证了统一评估协议在诊断导航性能方面的价值。

Conclusion: NavArena在大型3DGS重建上实现了可扩展且可复现的导航评估。基准生成工具、评估协议及相关资产将全部公开，以促进该领域研究。

Abstract: Fixed 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) reconstructions provide realistic novel views but lack the traversability constraints, valid goals, and closed-loop protocols required for navigation evaluation. We introduce NavArena, an automated framework that transforms fixed 3DGS reconstructions into benchmarks for goal-oriented visual navigation. NavArena integrates a frozen 3DGS model for egocentric RGB-D rendering, an occupancy costmap derived from Gaussian density and height statistics for reachability and collision queries, and semantic goal candidates lifted from multi-view open-vocabulary masks. These components support the automatic generation and unified closed-loop evaluation of goal-oriented navigation episodes. Across more than 2{,}000 scenes, NavArena generates 22.2 million expert trajectories. Spatial and semantic evaluations assess the derived navigation representations, while policy rollouts demonstrate the diagnostic value of the unified evaluation protocol. NavArena enables scalable and reproducible navigation evaluation on large-scale 3DGS reconstructions, and all benchmark-generation tools, evaluation protocols, and derived assets will be released publicly.

</details>


### [260] [Open-Set 3D Scene Graphs for Field Robotics: An Outdoor Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04607)
*Chad R. Samuelson,Gabriel R. Slade,Joshua G. Mangelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对室外三维场景图（3DSGs）与开放集视觉语言模型结合使用的行为进行了大规模实地分析，通过五个室外机器人数据集揭示了VLM点嵌入中存在显著异常值和多种语义模式等问题，验证了室外3DSG在导航目标检索中的可行性（成功率约70%），同时指出了可通行性建模和区域级理解等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DSGs作为支持高层机器人推理的通用层级地图表示已显示出潜力，但将其与开放集视觉语言模型（VLMs）结合用于真实室外环境时的行为特性尚未得到充分理解。需要对实际部署中的挑战进行系统分析，以指导未来3DSG的设计与改进。

Method: 以Terra 3DSG为案例，跨五个多样化的室外机器人数据集，分析3DSG的四大核心组件：语义点嵌入、地点节点图导航、区域级理解以及内存大小。引入新颖的一致性度量指标，评估在相同环境的重复遍历中语义和结构图属性是否保持稳定。量化VLM嵌入的异常值比率、导航路径效率、区域级F1分数及内存占用。

Result: 所有测试数据集中VLM点嵌入普遍存在异常值和多种模式，约30%的点异常值比率超过0.1。室外3DSG在导航目标检索中实现了接近70%的成功率，但轨迹平均有约66%的次优路径效率，受限于可通行性失败和低效路由。区域级理解在复杂自然环境中仍具挑战，平均F1分数约0.359。室外3DSG可对多公里轨迹保持紧凑（小于600MB）且相对一致的大规模环境表示。

Conclusion: 室外3DSG能够维持紧凑且相对一致的大规模环境表示，验证了其在导航目标检索任务中的可行性；但开放挑战依然存在，包括处理多种语义模式、将可通行性纳入图结构以及提升更高层次区域理解能力。

Abstract: Three-dimensional scene graphs (3DSGs) have emerged as a promising approach for building geometrically grounded, semantically informed, hierarchical general-purpose maps to support high-level robotic reasoning. However, the behavior of 3DSGs in real-world outdoor deployments remains poorly understood, particularly when combined with open-set vision-language models (VLMs). In this field report, we analyze the components common to most 3DSG representations across five outdoor robotic datasets to characterize challenges that arise in complex outdoor environments. Using the recently proposed Terra 3DSG as a case study, we investigate semantic point embeddings, place-node graph navigation, region-level understanding, and memory size across the five diverse datasets. We additionally introduce novel consistency metrics to evaluate whether semantic and structural graph properties remain stable across repeated traversals of the same environment. Our analysis reveals that outliers and multiple modes are common in VLM point embeddings across all tested datasets with outlier ratios above $0.1$ for around $30\%$ of points. We demonstrate the feasibility of outdoor 3DSGs for navigation-based object retrieval, achieving success rates near $70\%$, though performance is limited by traversability failures and inefficient routing, with trajectories averaging approximately $66\%$ suboptimal path efficiency. Region-level understanding remains challenging in complex natural environments, with low average F1 scores around $0.359$. Overall, our results show that outdoor 3DSGs can maintain compact (less than $600$MB for multi-kilometer trajectories) and relatively consistent large-scale environment representations, while highlighting open challenges in handling multiple semantic modes, incorporating traversability into graph structures, and improving higher-level region understanding.

</details>


### [261] [Pack It My Way: Triadic Human-Robot Collaboration for Personalized Autonomous Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04620)
*Sandeep Chowdary Kotapati,Yanxin Gao,Tsung-Chi Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了个性化自主装箱中的三方人机协作，比较了人类专家调解与语音代理调解两种方式在偏好纠正和泛化上的效果。结果表明，语音代理在两类偏好任务中达到与人类专家相当的表现，且两者易用性相近，但人类专家被认为更可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化自主装箱需要机器人理解无法仅从场景几何推断的居民偏好。虽然专家远程操作者可以解读这些偏好并将其转化为可行的机器人动作，但持续依赖专家会限制系统的可扩展部署，因此需要探索替代专家参与的调解方式。

Method: 研究设计了三方人机协作框架，包括居民、调解者和机器人，比较人类专家调解与语音代理调解两种条件。在保护性、紧凑性和分组性三类任务中开展用户研究，采用"展示-纠正-泛化"流程评估偏好纠正效果及周围物体重新排列后的泛化表现。

Result: 语音代理调解在三个偏好类别中的两个（保护性和紧凑性相关任务）达到与人类专家调解相当的结果，尽管收到的指令更短、更不详细。两种调解方式在易用性上评价相近，但人类专家被认为更为可靠。

Conclusion: 研究证明语音代理能够减少对专家的依赖，但感知可靠性和偏好泛化仍是剩余挑战，需要在后续工作中加以解决。

Abstract: Personalized autonomous packing requires robots to account for resident preferences that cannot be inferred from scene geometry alone. Expert teleoperators can interpret these preferences and translate them into feasible robot actions, but continuous expert involvement limits scalable deployment. In this paper, we investigate triadic human-robot collaboration among a resident, a correction mediator, and a robot by comparing human-expert and voice-agent mediation. We evaluate the two conditions in a user study across Protection, Compactness, andGrouping tasks, using a Show-Correct-Generalize process to assess preference correction and subsequent generalization after the surrounding objects are rearranged. Results show that voice-agent mediation achieves outcomes comparable to human-expert mediation in two of the three preference categories, despite receiving shorter and less detailed instructions. Both mediators are similarly easy to use, although the human expert is perceived as more reliable. These findings demonstrate the potential of voice agents to reduce expert involvement while identifying perceived reliability and preference generalization as remaining challenges.

</details>


### [262] [Dressing in Motion: A Human Motion-Aware Diffusion Policy for Robot-Assisted Dressing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04759)
*Haoxiang Sun,Fangyuan Wang,Songhao Huang,Justina Y. W. Liu,Jihong Zhu,Peng Zhou,David Navarro-Alarcon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种视觉运动策略，从静态专家演示中学习穿衣技能，并泛化到动态人体运动场景。该方法基于适应人衣交互几何的扩散策略，结合PDE扩散的物体中心表示捕捉手臂轴向分布，通过采样运动相关区域并跨帧配准来近似手臂运动并反应式调整轨迹。仿真与真实人类研究（9名参与者、3种服装、6种手臂运动模式）验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助穿衣是帮助身体受损老年人日常生活的有前景方案，但人体运动状态下的穿衣仍具挑战，因为复杂的人衣接触和遮挡使得难以生成与手臂运动对齐的动作。现有方法多依赖静态演示，难以应对动态用户运动场景。

Method: 1）提出一种面向人衣交互几何定制的扩散策略，从部分观察点云（含不同手臂姿态）中学习穿衣技能；2）引入基于PDE扩散的物体中心表示，捕捉手臂的轴向分布；3）通过采样运动相关区域并在连续观测间进行配准，近似手臂运动并实现轨迹的反应式自适应调整。

Result: 在仿真和真实人类研究中评估了该方法，涉及9名参与者、3种服装类型和6种手臂运动模式。结果显示该方法在穿衣进度、运动自由度和用户舒适度方面均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的视觉运动策略能够从静态演示中学习并在动态用户运动场景下有效泛化，实现了在人体运动过程中的鲁棒穿衣辅助，在关键评价指标上优于基线方法。

Abstract: Robotic dressing assistance is a promising solution for supporting older adults with physical impairments in daily living. However, dressing under human motion remains challenging, as complex garment--human contact and occlusions make it difficult to generate actions aligned with arm movements. In this letter, we propose a visuomotor policy that learns dressing skills from static expert demonstrations and generalizes to dynamic user-motion scenarios. A diffusion policy tailored to garment--human interaction geometry learns from partially observed point clouds with varied arm postures. We then introduce an object-centric representation based on PDE diffusion to capture the axial distribution of the arm. By sampling motion-relevant regions and registering them across consecutive observations, the proposed method approximates arm motion and reactively adapts the executed trajectory. We evaluate our method in simulation and a real-world human study involving nine participants, three garment types, and six arm-motion patterns. Results show that our method outperforms baselines in dressing progress, freedom of movement, and user comfort. The project website is https://anonymous.4open.science/w/dressing-in-motion.

</details>


### [263] [Continuous Cognitive Coverage for Autonomous Robots via Event-Dependent Cognitive Treatment and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04770)
*Hong Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种持续认知覆盖框架，使每个被认知接纳的事件都根据其状态、情境和历史获得差异化认知处理（如描述、记忆、风险预测、规划、诊断、类比等），支持熟悉事件自动处理、陌生事件显式推理，并通过经验持续学习实现自动化的处理完善与修订。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人会持续遭遇物体、变化与情境，每个被认知接纳的事件都应获得适当的认知处理，而非等到显式任务需要时才被关注。然而现有任务驱动、反应式或固定推理方法通常只处理选择性事件或应用预定义的推理程序，难以提供有区别处理的持续认知覆盖。

Method: 提出一种持续认知覆盖框架：(1) 每个被认知接纳的事件依据其状态、情境和历史被分配事件依赖的认知处理（描述、记忆、风险预测、规划、诊断、类比或学习到的处理）；(2) 熟悉事件通过学习机制自动处理，不熟悉或不确定的事件触发显式审议或回退推理；(3) 多个认知过程可被挂起、恢复和交错执行，确保新事件到达或已有事件等待证据时认知处理持续进行；(4) 持续学习验证过的经验以自动化、细化和修订事件特定处理。

Result: 实验达到96.76%的结构化处理准确率、93.66%的自动处理率、在突发延迟工作负载下92.64%的认知覆盖率、79.53%的持续学习联合准确率，以及新事件复用达100%的自动处理率。

Conclusion: 该持续认知覆盖框架能够为不同事件提供差异化的持续认知处理，并兼顾自动处理与显式推理的平衡，通过持续学习不断提升处理的自适应性和效率，实验结果验证了框架在准确率、覆盖率、抗突发延迟及持续学习方面的有效性。

Abstract: Autonomous robots continuously encounter objects, changes, and situations, and every event admitted into cognition should receive an appropriate cognitive treatment rather than remain untreated until an explicit task requires attention. However, existing task-driven, reactive, or fixed-reasoning approaches generally process only selected events or apply predefined reasoning procedures, making it difficult to provide continuous cognitive coverage with differentiated treatment. This paper proposes a continuous cognitive coverage framework in which every cognitively admitted event is assigned an event-dependent cognitive treatment according to its state, context, and history. Different events may therefore invoke description, memory, risk prediction, planning, diagnosis, analogy, or other learned treatments. Familiar events can be processed automatically by learned mechanisms, whereas unfamiliar or uncertain events invoke explicit deliberation or fallback reasoning. Multiple cognitive processes can be suspended, resumed, and interleaved so that cognitive processing continues as new events arrive or existing events await evidence. Validated experiences are continuously learned to automate, refine, and revise event-specific treatments. Experiments achieve 96.76% structured treatment accuracy with 93.66% automatic processing, 92.64% cognitive coverage under bursty-delayed workloads, and 79.53% continual-learning joint accuracy, with novel-event reuse reaching 100% automatic processing.

</details>


### [264] [HaptiNet: Networked Haptic Robots Enable Physical Co-presence in Geographically-Unconstrained Rehabilitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04799)
*Chenyang Sun,Mingjie Dong,Haodong Deng,Yudong Liu,Yi-Feng Chen,Jun Lin,Changlong Huang,Jie Guo,Yantong Liu,Yang Liu,Yuzhou Lin,Jianjun Long,Zheng Xing,Sining Zhao,Xuemin Zhang,Zhiyong Wang,Zhenhong Li,Dongrui Wu,Honghai Liu,Jian S. Dai,Mingming Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HaptiNet是一种面向远程康复的网络化触觉机器人系统，通过力介导交互实现地理分散用户间的物理共在。该系统具有低惯性、长行程、高力反馈能力的机械终端，并配有基于模仿学习的延迟补偿器。在284名健康受试者和111名神经功能障碍患者中的验证表明，触觉协作相比单独和视觉协作训练分别提升任务性能24%和22%，在约4000km的三城际链路中保持稳定交互，成绩改善为单独训练的3.87倍，患者主动用力提高106%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同康复需要物理共在，用户须通过触觉接触传递力、协调运动并推断意图，但现有远程康复技术以视听为主，使患者处于触觉和物理孤立状态。作者旨在突破地理、行动能力和临床差异带来的限制，为远程康复提供真实的身体共在体验。

Method: 1) 设计低惯性、长行程、高力反馈能力的机器人终端，适用于上肢训练的触觉渲染；2) 构建分布式触觉网络，引入基于模仿学习的延迟补偿器，实现远距离物理感知与协调；3) 在实验室测试、跨城部署和临床应用等递增真实情境下，对健康受试者及神经功能障碍患者进行系统验证。

Result: 284名健康参与者和111名神经功能障碍患者参与了验证。系统在单用户和多用户场景下保持任务级力渲染一致性。触觉协作相比单独训练和视觉协作训练，任务性能分别提升24%和22%，同时增强参与度和人际运动同步性。在总计约4000km的三条跨城链路中，神经功能障碍患者的触觉交互保持稳定，基线到训练的成绩改善为单独条件的3.87倍，患者主动用力提高106%。

Conclusion: HaptiNet证明了通过网络化触觉机器人系统，可实现远程康复中的力介导物理共在，弥补了当前远程康复在触觉与物理交互维度的缺失，为受距离和移动性限制的患者提供了更有效的康复训练途径。

Abstract: Cooperative rehabilitation enhances engagement, task performance, and social-motor interaction, yet it demands physical co-presence: users must transmit forces, coordinate movements, and infer intent through haptic contact. Telerehabilitation promises to expand access for patients constrained by distance, mobility, or clinical disparities, yet current techniques remain predominantly audiovisual while leaving users haptically and physically isolated. Here, we introduce HaptiNet, a networked haptic robotic system enabling physical co-presence for geographically distributed users via force-mediated interaction. Each robotic terminal features a low-inertia, long-stroke design with high force-feedback capacity, tailored for haptic rendering in upper-limb training. Building on these terminals, HaptiNet creates a distributed haptic network with an imitation-learning-based delay compensator, enabling users to physically perceive and coordinate with one another over distance. We validated HaptiNet in 284 healthy participants and 111 patients with neurological impairments across progressively realistic settings, including laboratory tests, cross-city deployments, and clinical applications. HaptiNet preserved task-level force rendering consistency across single-user and multi-user scenarios. Compared with solo and visual cooperative training, haptic cooperation improved task performance by 24% and 22%, respectively, while also boosting engagement and interpersonal motor synchrony. Across three intercity links totaling approximately 4,000 km, HaptiNet maintained stable haptic interaction among patients with neurological impairments, producing a 3.87-fold greater baseline-to-training score improvement and a 106% higher patient-applied effort over the solo condition.

</details>


### [265] [CoLMIN: LLM-based Multi-Decision Path Negotiation for Cooperative Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04807)
*Zhe Huang,Zhaoxin Fan,Shuo Wang,Wenjun Wu,Xuan Zhao,Min Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CoLMIN，一种基于LLM的多决策路径协商框架，用于多车协同自动驾驶。通过LMin模块生成多个候选驾驶意图进行联合评估，ESRM模块提供浅层反馈加速共识形成，LDRM模块进行深层反思防止认知固化，从而避免协商过程过早收敛到次优解。在CARLA仿真环境中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM协商的协同驾驶方法存在关键缺陷：复杂交通场景中的驾驶决策本质上是多解的，而现有协商方法往往过早收敛到次优解，阻碍了共识形成，限制了协同自动驾驶系统的实际部署。需要一种能够探索多个决策路径并形成稳定高质量共识的方法。

Method: CoLMIN包含三个核心组件：(i) LMin模块，采用Negotiator-Evaluator范式，生成多个候选驾驶意图进行联合评估；(ii) ESRM模块，分析协商结果并提供反馈以引导后续协商，加速共识形成；(iii) LDRM模块，对协商历史进行长期反思，以缓解认知固化并防止系统收敛到次优解。

Result: 在CARLA仿真环境中，CoLMIN在具有挑战性的交互式驾驶场景中显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过多决策路径协商和反思推理，CoLMIN能够在协同自动驾驶中实现稳定的决策共识，提高复杂交互场景下的协同驾驶性能。

Abstract: Multi-vehicle cooperative autonomous driving enhances the safety and reliability of autonomous driving systems through information sharing among connected vehicles, demonstrating significant potential for improving traffic safety. LLM-based approaches leverage strong reasoning capabilities of LLMs to enable effective inter-vehicle negotiation and improve cooperative driving performance. However, driving decisions in complex traffic scenarios are inherently multi-solution in nature. As a result, existing negotiation-based methods often converge prematurely to suboptimal solutions, hindering consensus formation and limiting the practical deployment of cooperative autonomous driving systems. To address this challenge, we propose CoLMIN, the LLM-based multi-decision path negotiation framework for cooperative autonomous driving, achieving stable decision consensus through multi-decision path negotiation and reflective reasoning. To achieve stable and high-quality consensus in cooperative autonomous driving, CoLMIN consists of three key components: (i) an LLM-based Multi-Intent Negotiation module (LMin), which adopts a Negotiator-Evaluator paradigm and generates multiple candidate driving intentions for joint evaluation; (ii) an Evaluation-based Shallow Reflection Module (ESRM), which analyzes negotiation outcomes and provides feedback to guide subsequent negotiations, thereby accelerating consensus formation; and (iii) an LLM-based Deep Reflection Module (LDRM), which performs long-term reflection over negotiation histories to mitigate cognitive fixation and prevent the system from converging to suboptimal solutions. Experimental results in the CARLA simulation environment demonstrate that CoLMIN significantly outperforms existing methods in challenging interactive driving scenarios.

</details>


### [266] [Strict Modes Everywhere - Bringing Order Into Dynamics of Mechanical Systems by a Potential Compatible With the Geodesic Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04817)
*Arne Sachtler,Alin Albu-Schäffer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文设计了一种势能函数，使保守机械系统中所有构型空间都被严格非线性模态密集填充，每个构型属于一个模态且每个模态经过公共平衡点。该势能可通过非线性弹性元件实现，从而让系统的大部分驱动力矩来自弹性元件而非执行器，实现高效的周期性轨迹执行。文章还设计了镇定控制器（稳定到目标模态）和摆动/补偿能量损耗的控制器，并在二自由度机械臂上进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 严格非线性正常模态通常只是构型空间中的孤立曲线，限制了其在实际机械系统中的适用范围。作者希望设计一种势能，使所有构型空间点都被严格正常模态覆盖，从而利用弹性元件高效执行多种周期性轨迹，减少对执行器的依赖。

Method: 1）设计一个特定形式的势能函数，使得系统的每个构型都落在一条通过平衡点的严格正常模态曲线上，从而实现对构型空间的密集填充。2）该势能可通过非线性弹性元件物理实现。3）设计两类控制器：一类用于将系统镇定到目标模态；另一类用于实现摆动（swing-up）并补偿能量耗散。4）在二自由度机械臂上进行实验验证。

Result: 在二自由度机械臂上的实验表明，所提方法在示例系统中表现良好，能够有效利用弹性元件执行周期性轨迹并稳定到目标模态。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了通过设计势能来构建由正常模态密集填充的构型空间的可能性，为高效、低执行器能耗的周期运动执行提供了新途径。实验验证了该方法的有效性和可行性。

Abstract: Strict nonlinear normal modes provide very regular families of oscillations within conservative mechanical systems. However, a strict normal mode will generally be an isolated curve within the configuration space of the system. In this letter, we design a potential that will densely fill the configuration space with strict normal modes such that each configuration belongs to one mode and each mode passes through a common point, the equilibrium.
